DESIGN.md

show
## Concept
The player controls a luminous dot that can be toggled between its normal size, half size, or double size for a brief period. Shrinking lets the dot slip through narrow gaps, while expanding lets it bypass small obstacles that would otherwise block it.

## Player
A simple glowing dot is the avatar. It can move around the play area and the player can trigger a size change (shrink or expand) that lasts for a few seconds.

## Core loop
Obstacles continuously approach the dot. The player watches the incoming patterns, decides whether to stay at normal size, shrink, or expand, and activates the size change at the right moment to avoid collision. This decision‑and‑reaction cycle repeats every few seconds.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- Narrow gaps that only a shrunken dot can pass.
- Small spikes or protrusions that a normal‑sized dot would hit but a doubled dot can push past.
- Moving walls with opening sizes that require the correct size to traverse.
- Timed laser sweeps that a shrunken dot can duck under.

## Score
Points are awarded each time an obstacle is successfully navigated. Consecutive successful passes build a combo multiplier, and surviving longer adds incremental bonuses.

## Win and lose
The player loses when colliding with an obstacle while at an inappropriate size; a Game Over overlay appears showing the final score and a restart prompt. The player wins by reaching a predefined distance or score target, triggering a celebratory visual and a victory message.

## Feel
Fast‑paced, reflex‑driven arcade action with a neon glow aesthetic and pulsing synth soundtrack. The bright dot against a dark background creates tension and flow, encouraging quick, satisfying size‑shift decisions.

## Why it's fun
The thrill of instantly reshaping yourself to fit impossible spaces creates a satisfying “aha!” moment each time you slip through a tight gap.

TASKS.md

show
1. HTML scaffold + meta.json — create `index.html` with required header, canvas, restart button, empty script block, and `meta.json` matching title, description, controls, tags.
2. Game state objects — define plain objects for player, hazards array, score, phase, constants (e.g., SAFE_RADIUS) at the top of the script.
3. Animation loop — implement `startLoop` with `cancelAnimationFrame` handling, requestAnimationFrame loop invoking `update(dt)` and `render()`.
4. Input handling — set up a `keys` Set via keydown/keyup listeners, R-key restart hook, and restart button click hook calling `reset()`.
5. Player movement — add dt‑based physics for player movement using arrow keys, with speed, friction, bounds, and update meta player position.
6. Size‑shift mechanic — allow toggling player size (shrink to 0.5× or expand to 2×) on tap/key for a few seconds, then revert.
7. Hazard system — design hazard data structure, spawn logic that respects `SAFE_RADIUS` and avoids spawning on the player, with dt‑scaled motion.
8. Collision detection — detect collisions between player (considering current size) and hazards; on hit transition to `gameover` state and stop the gameplay loop.
9. Scoring — increase score when hazards are successfully passed, and draw the score on the canvas top‑left.
10. Rendering — clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player (respecting current size), and HUD; ensure player is high‑contrast.
11. Game‑over overlay — dim background and draw "Game Over", final score, and restart instructions on the canvas.
12. Observable contract — populate `window.__game` each frame with `state`, `score`, player `x`, `y`, `visible`, and an `objective` sentence.
13. Audio feedback — implement lazy‑init `AudioContext` with a `beep()` helper, hooking into hit, score, and game‑over events.
14. Final polish — define all numeric constants (player speed, size‑shift duration, hazard speed, canvas size), ensure no uncaught exceptions, and verify controls affect gameplay.
15. Validation checklist — run quick sanity checks: page loads without errors, controls work, player visible, score updates, and game‑over triggers correctly.

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =========================================================
  let audioCtx = null;
function beep(freq, durSec) {
  try {
    if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
    const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
    osc.type = "square";
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
  } catch (e) {
    // ignore audio errors
  }
}

On restart you MUST `cancelAnimationFrame` any pending loop before starting a new one (otherwise loops stack on each restart and the game eventually slows to a crawl).\n95: 5. **Progressive difficulty** — start gentle so the player can learn, then ramp up. The player can lose\n105: \n106: There must be a collision check (or other end condition) that fires `gameOver` and stops the active gameplay loop. \"Game runs forever, no death\" is broken. Common interpretations:
- "canvas appears blank" → the game is loading but `render()` isn't drawing anything. Check that `startLoop()` actually got called at boot, that `render()` runs every frame, and that you're issuing `ctx.fill*` / `ctx.stroke*` calls per entity each frame (not just clearing the canvas).

// === AUDIO (lazy) =========================================================
let audioCtx = null;
function beep(freq, durSec) {
  try {
    if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
    const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
    osc.type = "square";
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
  } catch (e) {
    // ignore audio errors
  }
}

// === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ================================================
  // TODO: publish __game state

// === BOOT ================================================================
  // TODO: start the game

```
function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      update(dt);
      render();
      publishState();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('loop error', err);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}
```

```
function update(dt) {
  if (phase !== 'playing') return;

  // Player movement
  const moveLeft = keys.has('ArrowLeft') || keys.has('a') || keys.has('A');
  const moveRight = keys.has('ArrowRight') || keys.has('d') || keys.has('D');
  const dir = (moveRight ? 1 : 0) - (moveLeft ? 1 : 0);
  player.x += dir * PLAYER_SPEED * dt;
  // Clamp within canvas
  player.x = clamp(player.x, player.radius * player.sizeMult, canvas.width - player.radius * player.sizeMult);
  
  // Size shift timer
  if (sizeShiftTimer > 0) {
    sizeShiftTimer -= dt;
    if (sizeShiftTimer <= 0) {
      player.sizeMult = 1;
    }
  }

  // Hazard spawning
  hazardSpawnTimer -= dt;
  if (hazardSpawnTimer <= 0) {
    // spawn hazard off right edge
    const y = Math.random() * (canvas.height - 2 * HAZARD_RADIUS) + HAZARD_RADIUS;
    hazards.push({ x: canvas.width + HAZARD_RADIUS, y, radius: HAZARD_RADIUS });
    hazardSpawnTimer = SPAWN_INTERVAL;
  }

  // Move hazards
  for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const h = hazards[i];
    h.x -= HAZARD_SPEED * dt;
    // Collision detection
    const dx = h.x - player.x;
    const dy = h.y - player.y;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    if (dist < (h.radius + player.radius * player.sizeMult)) {
      phase = 'gameover';
      beep(110, 0.25);
      // keep hazard for rendering
    } else if (h.x + h.radius < 0) {
      // Hazard passed, increase score
      score += 1;
      beep(880, 0.05);
      hazards.splice(i, 1);
    }
  }
}

// === RENDER =============================================================
function render() {
  // Clear background
  ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  
  // Draw hazards
  ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
  for (const h of hazards) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(h.x, h.y, h.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }
  
  // Draw player
  ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius * player.sizeMult, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
  
  // HUD
  ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
  ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
  ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
  
  // Game over overlay
  if (phase === 'gameover') {
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(10,10,15,0.7)";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
    ctx.fillText("Game Over", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 20);
    ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 14);
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
    ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 42);
  }
}
```

document.addEventListener('keydown', (ev) => {

Let's design the final script outline:

// === CONFIG =============================================================
const SAFE_RADIUS = 200; // safe spawn radius around player start
const SIZE_SHIFT_DURATION = 2; // seconds duration of size shift
// Gameplay constants
const PLAYER_SPEED = 300; // px/sec
const PLAYER_BASE_RADIUS = 20;
const HAZARD_SPEED = 200; // px/sec
const HAZARD_RADIUS = 15;
const SPAWN_INTERVAL = 1.5; // seconds between hazards

// === STATE ==============================================================
const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");
let player = { x: 400, y: 300, radius: PLAYER_BASE_RADIUS, sizeMult: 1 };
let hazards = []; // array of hazard objects
let score = 0;
let phase = "menu"; // "menu" | "playing" | "gameover"
let keys = new Set();
let loopId = null;
let lastTime = performance.now();
let spawnTimer = SPAWN_INTERVAL;
let sizeShiftTimer = 0;
let sizeShiftMode = "normal";

function reset() {
  player.x = canvas.width / 2;
  player.y = canvas.height / 2;
  player.sizeMult = 1;
  hazards = [];
  score = 0;
  phase = "playing";
  keys.clear();
  spawnTimer = SPAWN_INTERVAL;
  sizeShiftTimer = 0;
  sizeShiftMode = "normal";
  lastTime = performance.now();
  publishState();
  startLoop();
}

// === LOOP ==============================================================
function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      update(dt);
      render();
      publishState();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("loop error", err);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

Implement input:

document.addEventListener("keydown", ev => {
  if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
  keys.add(ev.key);
});

document.addEventListener("keyup", ev => {
  keys.delete(ev.key);
});

canvas.addEventListener("click", ev => {
  // toggle size shift
  if (sizeShiftMode === "normal") {
    player.sizeMult = 0.5;
    sizeShiftMode = "shrink";
  } else if (sizeShiftMode === "shrink") {
    player.sizeMult = 2;
    sizeShiftMode = "expand";
  } else {
    player.sizeMult = 1;
    sizeShiftMode = "normal";
  }
  sizeShiftTimer = SIZE_SHIFT_DURATION;
  beep(880, 0.05); // maybe sound
});

restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

Maybe also handle touch events: canvas.addEventListener("touchend", ...). For now click is fine.

Now update function details:

function update(dt) {
  if (phase !== "playing") return;

  // size shift timer
  if (sizeShiftTimer > 0) {
    sizeShiftTimer -= dt;
    if (sizeShiftTimer <= 0) {
      player.sizeMult = 1;
      sizeShiftMode = "normal";
      sizeShiftTimer = 0;
    }
  }

  // movement
  let dirX = 0, dirY = 0;
  if (keys.has("ArrowLeft") || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A")) dirX -= 1;
  if (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D")) dirX += 1;
  if (keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W")) dirY -= 1;
  if (keys.has("ArrowDown") || keys.has("s") || keys.has("S")) dirY += 1;
  if (dirX !== 0 || dirY !== 0) {
    const len = Math.hypot(dirX, dirY);
    dirX /= len;
    dirY /= len;
    player.x += dirX * PLAYER_SPEED * dt;
    player.y += dirY * PLAYER_SPEED * dt;
    // clamp
    player.x = Math.max(player.radius * player.sizeMult, Math.min(canvas.width - player.radius * player.sizeMult, player.x));
    player.y = Math.max(player.radius * player.sizeMult, Math.min(canvas.height - player.radius * player.sizeMult, player.y));
  }

  // spawn hazards
  spawnTimer -= dt;
  if (spawnTimer <= 0) {
    // spawn a hazard on left side, random y not too close to player start if within safe radius
    const y = Math.random() * canvas.height;
    const x = -HAZARD_RADIUS;
    hazards.push({ x, y, vx: HAZARD_SPEED, vy: 0, radius: HAZARD_RADIUS });
    spawnTimer = SPAWN_INTERVAL;
  }

  // update hazards
  for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const h = hazards[i];
    h.x += h.vx * dt;
    // remove off-screen right
    if (h.x - h.radius > canvas.width) {
      hazards.splice(i, 1);
      score++;
      beep(660, 0.03);
      continue;
    }

    // collision
    const dx = h.x - player.x;
    const dy = h.y - player.y;
    const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
    const radSum = (player.radius * player.sizeMult) + h.radius;
    if (distSq < radSum * radSum) {
      phase = "gameover";
      beep(110, 0.25);
      // no break, allow other hazards removal?
    }
  }
}

Now render function:

function render() {
  // clear
  ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  // draw hazards
  ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
  for (const h of hazards) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(h.x, h.y, h.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }

  // draw player
  ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius * player.sizeMult, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  // HUD
  ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
  ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
  ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);

  if (phase === "gameover") {
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(10,10,15,0.7)";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
    ctx.fillText("Game Over", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 20);
    ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 14);
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
    ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 42);
  }
}

Now publishState function:

function publishState() {
  window.__game = {
    state: phase === "menu" ? "menu" : phase === "playing" ? "playing" : "gameover",
    score,
    player: {
      x: player.x,
      y: player.y,
      visible: player.x >= 0 && player.x <= canvas.width && player.y >= 0 && player.y <= canvas.height,
    },
    objective: "Tap to toggle size to avoid hazards and survive as long as possible.",
  };
}

prompt (13,908 chars)
## Reference example: "breakout"

The following is a complete, validator-passing game. **Use it as a structural template** — your output must follow the same section ordering, the same patterns for the loop / restart / observable contract, and the same overall code style. Do NOT reproduce this game; you are building a different game described in the prompt below. The example exists only to anchor structure.

Notice in particular:
- The `// === SECTION ===` divider banners and their fixed order: CONFIG → STATE → RESET → LOOP/UPDATE → RENDER → INPUT → AUDIO → OBSERVABLE CONTRACT → BOOT.
- Plain object literals for game state (no `class`).
- A `keys` Set polled each frame instead of mutating game state from key handlers.
- `if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` at the top of `startLoop()`.
- The animation loop body wrapped in `try/catch`.
- `publishState()` called once per frame at the end of the tick.
- `window.__game` populated honestly (player.visible reflects actual viewport position).
- AudioContext initialized lazily inside `beep()`, wrapped in try/catch.

### Example `meta.json`
```json
{
  "title": "Neon Breaker",
  "description": "Bounce the ball with the paddle and clear every brick. One ball, no second chances.",
  "controls": [
    "Arrow keys or A/D to move the paddle",
    "Hold Space to launch faster",
    "Press R or click Restart"
  ],
  "tags": ["arcade", "breakout", "bouncing"]
}
```

### Example `index.html`
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Neon Breaker</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #0f1320; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Neon Breaker</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Bounce the ball with the paddle and clear every brick. One ball, no second chances.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Arrow keys or A/D to move the paddle</li>
      <li>Hold Space to launch faster</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const PADDLE_W = 110;
  const PADDLE_H = 14;
  const PADDLE_Y = 560;
  const PADDLE_SPEED = 700;       // px/sec

  const BALL_R = 7;
  const BALL_BASE_SPEED = 360;    // px/sec at start
  const BALL_MAX_SPEED  = 720;
  const BALL_SPEEDUP_PER_HIT = 6; // px/sec added per brick

  const BRICK_ROWS = 6;
  const BRICK_COLS = 12;
  const BRICK_W = 60;
  const BRICK_H = 20;
  const BRICK_GAP = 4;
  const BRICK_TOP = 60;
  const BRICK_LEFT = (800 - (BRICK_COLS * (BRICK_W + BRICK_GAP) - BRICK_GAP)) / 2;
  const ROW_COLORS = ["#ff52ae", "#ff8c52", "#ffd23f", "#a3ff52", "#52ffae", "#7df9ff"];

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

  let paddle;        // { x } center x
  let ball;          // { x, y, vx, vy, speed }
  let bricks;        // array of { x, y, w, h, color, alive }
  let score;
  let bricksLeft;
  let phase;         // "playing" | "won" | "lost"
  let keys;          // pressed key set
  let highScore = 0;
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  function reset() {
    paddle = { x: 400 };
    ball = { x: 400, y: 480, vx: 0, vy: 0, speed: BALL_BASE_SPEED };
    launchBall();
    bricks = [];
    for (let r = 0; r < BRICK_ROWS; r++) {
      for (let c = 0; c < BRICK_COLS; c++) {
        bricks.push({
          x: BRICK_LEFT + c * (BRICK_W + BRICK_GAP),
          y: BRICK_TOP + r * (BRICK_H + BRICK_GAP),
          w: BRICK_W, h: BRICK_H,
          color: ROW_COLORS[r % ROW_COLORS.length],
          alive: true,
        });
      }
    }
    bricksLeft = bricks.length;
    score = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  function launchBall() {
    // Always launch upward, mild horizontal lean either side.
    const angle = (-Math.PI / 2) + (Math.random() * 0.6 - 0.3);
    ball.vx = Math.cos(angle) * ball.speed;
    ball.vy = Math.sin(angle) * ball.speed;
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("loop error", err);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  // === UPDATE =============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== "playing") return;

    // Paddle.
    const dirX = (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D") ? 1 : 0)
               - (keys.has("ArrowLeft")  || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A") ? 1 : 0);
    const speedMul = keys.has(" ") ? 1.6 : 1;
    paddle.x += dirX * PADDLE_SPEED * speedMul * dt;
    paddle.x = clamp(paddle.x, PADDLE_W / 2, canvas.width - PADDLE_W / 2);

    // Ball motion.
    ball.x += ball.vx * dt;
    ball.y += ball.vy * dt;

    // Walls.
    if (ball.x < BALL_R) { ball.x = BALL_R; ball.vx = Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(220, 0.04); }
    if (ball.x > canvas.width - BALL_R) { ball.x = canvas.width - BALL_R; ball.vx = -Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(220, 0.04); }
    if (ball.y < BALL_R) { ball.y = BALL_R; ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy); beep(220, 0.04); }

    // Floor — lose.
    if (ball.y > canvas.height + BALL_R) {
      phase = "lost";
      if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
      beep(110, 0.25);
      return;
    }

    // Paddle.
    const px = paddle.x, py = PADDLE_Y;
    if (ball.vy > 0
        && ball.y + BALL_R >= py
        && ball.y - BALL_R <= py + PADDLE_H
        && ball.x >= px - PADDLE_W / 2 - BALL_R
        && ball.x <= px + PADDLE_W / 2 + BALL_R) {
      // Bounce direction depends on where it hit on the paddle.
      const offset = (ball.x - px) / (PADDLE_W / 2); // -1..1
      const angle = (-Math.PI / 2) + offset * 1.0;   // up-ish, leaning side
      ball.vx = Math.cos(angle) * ball.speed;
      ball.vy = Math.sin(angle) * ball.speed;
      ball.y = py - BALL_R - 0.1;
      beep(540, 0.05);
    }

    // Bricks.
    for (const b of bricks) {
      if (!b.alive) continue;
      if (ball.x + BALL_R < b.x || ball.x - BALL_R > b.x + b.w) continue;
      if (ball.y + BALL_R < b.y || ball.y - BALL_R > b.y + b.h) continue;

      b.alive = false;
      bricksLeft--;
      score += 10;
      ball.speed = Math.min(BALL_MAX_SPEED, ball.speed + BALL_SPEEDUP_PER_HIT);

      // Decide which side was hit by comparing penetrations.
      const overlapX = Math.min(ball.x + BALL_R - b.x, b.x + b.w - (ball.x - BALL_R));
      const overlapY = Math.min(ball.y + BALL_R - b.y, b.y + b.h - (ball.y - BALL_R));
      if (overlapX < overlapY) ball.vx = -ball.vx; else ball.vy = -ball.vy;

      // Renormalize to current speed.
      const m = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
      ball.vx = (ball.vx / m) * ball.speed;
      ball.vy = (ball.vy / m) * ball.speed;
      beep(880, 0.05);
      break;
    }

    if (bricksLeft === 0) {
      phase = "won";
      if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
      beep(990, 0.2);
    }
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

    // Bricks.
    for (const b of bricks) {
      if (!b.alive) continue;
      ctx.fillStyle = b.color;
      ctx.fillRect(b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h);
    }

    // Paddle.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
    ctx.fillRect(paddle.x - PADDLE_W / 2, PADDLE_Y, PADDLE_W, PADDLE_H);

    // Ball.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, BALL_R, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();

    // HUD.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    ctx.textAlign = "right";
    ctx.fillText(`Bricks ${bricksLeft}  ·  Best ${highScore}`, canvas.width - 12, 22);

    if (phase === "lost" || phase === "won") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(10,10,15,0.7)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillStyle = phase === "won" ? "#52ffae" : "#ff52ae";
      ctx.fillText(phase === "won" ? "You Win!" : "Game Over", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 42);
    }
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
  });
  document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
  restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = "square";
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    window.__game = {
      state: phase === "playing" ? "playing" : "gameover",
      score,
      player: {
        x: paddle.x,
        y: PADDLE_Y + PADDLE_H / 2,
        visible: paddle.x >= 0 && paddle.x <= canvas.width,
      },
      objective: "Bounce the ball with the paddle to break every brick.",
    };
  }

  // === HELPERS ============================================================
  function clamp(v, lo, hi) { return v < lo ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v); }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

Make startLoop as per reference:

function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      update(dt);
      render();
      publishState();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("loop error", err);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

Important: publishState and update must be defined.

Replace with block:

```
  // Keyboard input
  document.addEventListener('keydown', (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
  });
  document.addEventListener('keyup', (ev) => {
    keys.delete(ev.key);
  });
  // Restart button
  restartBtn.addEventListener('click', reset);
  // Size‑shift toggle on canvas click/tap
  canvas.addEventListener('click', () => {
    // Cancel any existing timeout
    if (sizeShiftTimeout !== null) {
      clearTimeout(sizeShiftTimeout);
      sizeShiftTimeout = null;
    }
    // Toggle size: shrink then expand alternately
    if (sizeShiftNext) {
      player.sizeMult = 0.5; // shrink
    } else {
      player.sizeMult = 2;   // expand
    }
    sizeShiftNext = !sizeShiftNext;
    // Revert after duration
    sizeShiftTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
      player.sizeMult = 1;
      sizeShiftTimeout = null;
    }, SIZE_SHIFT_DURATION * 1000);
  });
```

Add code:

let audioCtx = null;

function beep(freq, duration) {
  try {
    if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
    const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
    osc.type = "square";
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + duration);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + duration);
  } catch (e) {
    // ignore audio errors
  }
}

Add this inside the appropriate section.

prompt (5,157 chars)
Read CONVENTIONS.md and DESIGN.md in your working directory before doing anything else.

Your job in this stage is to break the implementation into a numbered list of small, ordered tasks. The next stage will work through this list one task at a time, in a single LLM session, reloading the page after each task and capturing console errors for you to fix.

Title: Size Shift
Concept: Tap to temporarily shrink the dot to half size or expand it to double size for a few seconds, letting you slip through narrow gaps or become too large for small obstacles that then pass harmlessly.

**You must produce one output file: `TASKS.md` in the working directory.** Use your write/edit tool to create this file on disk. After your turn ends, the validator runs `readFile("TASKS.md")` — if the file does not exist on disk, validation fails and your work is wasted. **Do NOT reply with the task list as inline chat text.** The list must be written to the file.

**Tool calls MUST be emitted as actual function/tool invocations — NOT described or simulated inside your reasoning/analysis text.** Writing tool-shaped JSON like `{"filePath":"TASKS.md","content":"..."}` *inside your chain-of-thought* does NOT invoke any tool. It is just text in the analysis channel that nothing reads or executes. The same goes for `{"pattern":"**/*"}` etc. — those only do something when emitted as a real `tool_use` event from the action channel, never when written into your thinking. Several prior runs have failed by hallucinating tool calls inside reasoning; do not repeat that pattern. If at any point you "describe" a tool call in your thinking instead of invoking it, you must immediately invoke the real tool afterward. The format MUST be a numbered list, one task per line, each line starting with `<number>.`:

```
1. <Task title> — <one-sentence description of what to do>
2. <Task title> — <one-sentence description>
...
```

The next stage parses tasks by matching `^\d+\.` at the start of each line. Don't use bullet sub-lists, don't number sub-tasks (1.1, 1.2). One number, one task, one line.

### What makes a good task

- **Small enough** to complete in a single coding turn (~50–150 lines of edits).
- **Testable**: after this task, what does the page do that it didn't before?
- **Leaves the file runnable**: at the end of the task, no syntax errors, no uncaught exceptions on load. Even if the game isn't playable yet, it must load.
- **Specific**: don't say "implement player". Say "implement player movement: arrow keys set keys.left/right; in update(), apply acceleration in the pressed direction with cap at MAX_SPEED, friction otherwise; clamp x to canvas bounds."
- **Concrete numbers**: this is where pixel sizes, ms intervals, and px/sec speeds live. Pick them. The implement stage will use them verbatim.

### Required coverage (in roughly this order)

Cover at minimum, ordered as below. Add tasks if the design demands more (powerups, level progression, screen wrap, etc):

1. **HTML scaffold + meta.json**: header (h1, description, controls list), canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md. Stub `<script>` block (empty). meta.json matching header text.
2. **Game state objects**: player, hazards, score, phase using plain object literals at the top of the script. Set initial values.
3. **requestAnimationFrame loop**: startLoop() with `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` discipline at the top. Body wrapped in try/catch. Calls update(dt) and render(). Don't draw anything yet besides clearing the canvas.
4. **Input**: a `keys` Set populated by document keydown/keyup. R-key restart hook. Restart button click hook. Both call the same reset() function.
5. **Player movement**: dt-based physics tied to the key inputs from task 4. Verify each control listed in meta.json actually changes a value.
6. **Hazards**: data structure, spawn rules with a SAFE_RADIUS so nothing spawns on top of the player at t=0, motion (dt-scaled).
7. **Collision detection**: player vs hazards. On hit, transition phase to "gameover" and stop the active gameplay.
8. **Score**: discrete events that increase score, drawn on canvas at top-left.
9. **Render**: clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player, HUD. Player must be the highest-contrast object on screen.
10. **Game-over overlay**: dim background, "Game Over", final score, "Press R or click Restart".
11. **Observable contract**: populate `window.__game = { state, score, player:{x,y,visible}, objective }` every frame. `state` is "playing" | "gameover". `objective` is a 10–200 char single-sentence description.
12. **Audio**: lazy-init AudioContext inside a beep() helper, wrapped in try/catch. Hook into hit / score / death events.

You may merge two trivial tasks if they truly belong together (e.g. tasks 1+2 if state is just three lines). You may split a complex task in two. Aim for 10–15 tasks total.

**Final check before you stop**: confirm you actually called your write/edit tool with `TASKS.md` as the path. If you only described the task list in chat without invoking the tool, the file will not exist on disk and validation will fail. After the file is written, stop. Do not begin implementation.
